2026-10-24T17:52:47.2164512Z class LanguageInstallationTask(Task):
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2165178Z     """Installation task for the language configuration."""

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2166342Z     LOCALE_CONF_FILE_PATH = "/etc/locale.conf"

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2168668Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2169024Z         :param sysroot: a path to the root of the installed system
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2169820Z         :param lang: a value for LANG locale variable

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2170834Z         super().__init__()

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2172228Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2172446Z     @property
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2172911Z     def name(self):
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2173389Z         return "Configure language"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2173801Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2174018Z     def run(self):
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2174515Z         """Run the installation task."""
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2175149Z         lang = self._get_supported_language()
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2175790Z         self._write_language_configuration(lang)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2176525Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2176858Z     def _get_supported_language(self):
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2182909Z         """Return a supported locale.
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2183366Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2183603Z         :return: a locale
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2186791Z         """
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2187377Z         if self._is_language_support_installed(self._lang):
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2188034Z             return self._lang
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2188392Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2193379Z         log.debug("The '%s' locale is unsupported.", self._lang)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2194294Z         log.debug("Using the '%s' locale as a fallback.", self.LOCALE_FALLBACK)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2194944Z         return self.LOCALE_FALLBACK
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2195268Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2195574Z     def _is_language_support_installed(self, lang):
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2197195Z         """Is the support for the specified language installed?
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2197937Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2198584Z         The language is considered to be supported if we are not
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2199498Z         able to determine the supported locales due to missing tools.
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2200089Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2200681Z         :param lang: a value for the LANG locale variable
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2201583Z         :return: False if the locale is known to be not supported, otherwise True
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2202365Z         """
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2202797Z         try:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2203372Z             output = execWithCapture("locale", ["-a"], root=self._sysroot)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2204088Z         except OSError as e:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2204675Z             log.warning("Couldn't get supported locales: %s", e)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2205310Z             return True
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2205660Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2206258Z         match = find_best_locale_match(lang, output.splitlines())
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2206840Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2207201Z         log.debug("The '%s' locale matched '%s'.", lang, match)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2207873Z         return bool(match)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2208231Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2208532Z     def _write_language_configuration(self, lang):
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2209299Z         """Write language configuration to the /etc/locale.conf file.
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2209878Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2210205Z         :param lang: a value for the LANG locale variable
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2210829Z         """
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2211229Z         try:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2211808Z             fpath = join_paths(self._sysroot, self.LOCALE_CONF_FILE_PATH)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2212625Z             log.debug("Writing the '%s' locale to %s.", lang, fpath)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2213188Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2213482Z             with open(fpath, "w") as fobj:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2214136Z                 fobj.write('LANG="{}"\n'.format(lang))
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2214633Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2214889Z         except OSError as e:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2215657Z             msg = "Cannot write language configuration file: {}".format(e.strerror)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2216910Z             raise LanguageInstallationError(msg) from e
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2217403Z

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2293446Z         # "eurlatgr" may not be the best choice for all the languages,

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2300155Z             # systemd now defaults to a font that cannot display non-ascii
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2300987Z             # characters, so we have to tell it to use a better one
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2301707Z             fobj.write('FONT="%s"\n' % vc_font)

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2302455Z     except OSError as e:

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2304040Z         raise KeyboardInstallationError(msg) from e

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2482082Z class CompositorLocaledWrapper(LocaledWrapperBase):
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2482878Z     """Localed wrapper class which is used to control compositor.
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2483438Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2483946Z     It adds support for layout selection and reactions on the compositor system changes.
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2484762Z     """
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2485018Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2485253Z     def __init__(self):
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2485716Z         super().__init__()
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2486308Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2486597Z         self._user_layouts_variants = []
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2487179Z         self._last_layouts_variants = []
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2487587Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2487901Z         self.compositor_layouts_changed = Signal()
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2488947Z         self.compositor_selected_layout_changed = Signal()
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2489508Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2489796Z         # to reflect updates from the compositor
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2490828Z         self._localed_proxy.PropertiesChanged.connect(self._on_properties_changed)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2491503Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2491966Z     def _on_properties_changed(self, interface, changed_props, invalid_props):
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2492900Z         if "X11Layout" in changed_props or "X11Variant" in changed_props:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2497110Z             layouts_variants = self._from_localed_format(changed_props["X11Layout"].get_string(),
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2498166Z                                                          changed_props["X11Variant"].get_string())
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2499096Z             # This part is a bit tricky. The signal processing here means that compositor has
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2500922Z             # changed current layouts configuration. This could happen for multiple reasons:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2501913Z             # - user changed the layout in compositor
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2502626Z             # - Anaconda set the layout to compositor
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2503428Z             # - any other magic logic for compositor (we just don't know)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2504164Z             #
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2504674Z             # The question is how we should behave:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2505525Z             # - we don't want to take compositor layouts to Anaconda because that will change
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2506712Z             #   what user will have in the installed system.
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2507565Z             # - we don't want to force our layouts to compositor because that would forbid user
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2508579Z             #   to change compositor layout when Anaconda runs in background
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2509301Z             #
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2510026Z             # The best shot seems to just signal out that the layout has changed and nothing else.
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2510728Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2511112Z             # layouts has changed in compositor, always emit this signal
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2512032Z             log.debug("Localed layouts has changed. Last known: '%s' current: '%s'",
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2512971Z                       self._last_layouts_variants, layouts_variants)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2513745Z             self.compositor_layouts_changed.emit(layouts_variants)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2514325Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2514663Z             # check if last selected variant has changed
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2515370Z             # nothing is selected in compositor
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2516241Z             if not layouts_variants:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2516946Z                 log.warning("Compositor layouts not set.")
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2517717Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit("")
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2518419Z             # we don't know last used layouts
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2519035Z             elif not self._last_layouts_variants:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2519730Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different. "
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2520521Z                           "Missing information about last selected layouts.")
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2521427Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2522211Z             # selected (first) has changed
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2522908Z             elif layouts_variants[0] != self._last_layouts_variants[0]:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2523727Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different.")
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2524600Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2525217Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2525526Z             self._last_layouts_variants = layouts_variants
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2526253Z

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2549917Z     def _set_layouts(self, layouts_variants, options=None, convert=False):
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2556532Z         """Set a new layouts for compositor.
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2556986Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2557302Z         Extension of the parent code with compositor specific code.
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2557927Z         """
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2558538Z         # If options are not set let's use from a system so we don't change the system settings
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2558967Z         if options is None:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2559400Z             options = self.options
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2559953Z             log.debug(

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2561727Z             )
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2561968Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2566706Z         # Disable keyboard layout switching by keyboard shortcut as it is hard to make this
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2567691Z         # working from the system to localed. The current layout is in general problematic to
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2568283Z         # decide.
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2568728Z         log.debug(
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2569436Z             "Disable keyboard layouts switching shortcut to compositor from the options: '%s'",

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2573562Z     def select_layout(self, layout_variant):
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2576971Z         """Select layout from the list of current layouts set.
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2577527Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2578002Z         This will search for the given layout variant in the list and move it as first
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2578804Z         in the list. The first layout in systemd is taken as the used one.
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2579359Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2579847Z         :param layout_variant: The layout to set, with format "layout (variant)"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2580687Z             (e.g. "cz (qwerty)")
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2581326Z         :type layout_variant: str
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2582054Z         :return: If the keyboard layout was activated
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2582724Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2583204Z         """
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2583795Z         # ignore compositor layouts but force Anaconda configuration
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2584583Z         layouts = self._user_layouts_variants
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2585057Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2585305Z         try:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2585877Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(layouts, layout_variant)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2587002Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2587564Z             return True
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2588044Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2588707Z             log.warning("Can't set layout: '%s' as first to the current set: %s",
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2589503Z                         layout_variant, layouts)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2590110Z             return False
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2590468Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2590704Z     @staticmethod
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2591229Z     def _shift_list(source_layouts, value_to_first):
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2592076Z         """Helper method to reorder list of layouts and move one as first in the list.
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2592716Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2593189Z         We should preserve the ordering just shift items from start of the list to the
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2594012Z         end in the same order.
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2594388Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2594694Z         When we want to set 2nd as first in this list:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2595299Z         ["cz", "es", "us"]
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2595787Z         The result should be:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2597042Z         ["es", "us", "cz"]
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2597443Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2597847Z         So the compositor has the same next layout as Anaconda.
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2599011Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2599485Z         :raises: ValueError: if the list is small or the layout is not inside
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2600249Z         """
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2600743Z         value_id = source_layouts.index(value_to_first)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2601633Z         new_list = source_layouts[value_id:len(source_layouts)] + source_layouts[0:value_id]
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2602469Z         return new_list
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2602795Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2603046Z     def select_next_layout(self):
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2603675Z         """Select (make it first) next layout for compositor.
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2604170Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2604668Z         Find current compositor layout in the list of defined layouts and set next to it as
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2605787Z         current (first) for compositor. We need to have user defined list because compositor
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2607239Z         layouts will change with the selection. Store this list when user is setting configuration
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2608235Z         to compositor. This list must not change ordering.
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2608744Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2609147Z         :param user_layouts: List of layouts selected by user in Anaconda.
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2609896Z         :type user_layouts: [str]
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2610745Z         :return: If switch was successful True otherwise False
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2611429Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2611858Z         """
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2612328Z         current_layout = self.current_layout_variant
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2613122Z         layout_id = 0
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2613440Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2613703Z         if not self._user_layouts_variants:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2614542Z             log.error("Can't switch next layout - user defined keyboard layout is not present!")
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2615383Z             return False
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2615717Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2615945Z         # find next layout
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2616957Z         for i, v in enumerate(self._user_layouts_variants):
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2617591Z             if v == current_layout:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2618133Z                 layout_id = i + 1
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2618715Z                 layout_id %= len(self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2619201Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2619418Z         try:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2619983Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(self._user_layouts_variants,
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2620767Z                                            self._user_layouts_variants[layout_id])
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2621510Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2622092Z             return True
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2622602Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2623331Z             log.warning("Can't set next keyboard layout %s", self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2624172Z             return False

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2812683Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2812773Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2813252Z def get_missing_keyboard_configuration(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2813564Z     """Get keyboard configuration if not set by user.
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2813654Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2813898Z     Algorithm works as this:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2814221Z     1. Check if full keyboard configuration is already set
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2814458Z        -> return these
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2814829Z     2. If no X layouts, check if they can be obtained from live system
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2815072Z        -> read X layouts
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2815355Z     3a. If still no configuration is present
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2815745Z        -> use DEFAULT_KEYBOARD as console layout and fall through to...
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2816489Z     3b. If only one of the keyboard layout or virtual console keymap is set
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2816798Z        -> convert one to the other by localed
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2816899Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2817314Z     :param localed_wrapper: instance of systemd-localed service wrapper
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2817584Z     :type localed_wrapper: LocaledWrapper
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2817901Z     :param x_layouts: list of X layout specifications
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2818143Z     :type x_layouts: list(str)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2818482Z     :param vc_keymap: virtual console keyboard mapping name
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2818720Z     :type vc_keymap: str
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2819037Z     :returns: tuple of X layouts and VC keyboard settings
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2819289Z     :rtype: (list(str), str))
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2819505Z     """
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2819749Z     if vc_keymap and x_layouts:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2820260Z         log.debug("Keyboard layouts and virtual console keymap already set - nothing to do")
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2820524Z         return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2820615Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2821087Z     live_keyboard = get_live_keyboard_instance()
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2821614Z     # layouts are not set by user, we should take a look for live configuration if available
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2822046Z     if not x_layouts and live_keyboard:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2822449Z         log.debug("Keyboard configuration from Live system is available")
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2822777Z         x_layouts = live_keyboard.read_keyboard_layouts()
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2822889Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2823154Z     if not vc_keymap and not x_layouts:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2823683Z         log.debug("Using default value %s for missing virtual console keymap", DEFAULT_KEYBOARD)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2823951Z         vc_keymap = DEFAULT_KEYBOARD
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2824048Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2824260Z     if not vc_keymap or not x_layouts:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2824599Z         x_layouts, vc_keymap = _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2824674Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2824843Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2824916Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2824985Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2825255Z def _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2825408Z     if not vc_keymap:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2825627Z         vc_keymap = localed_wrapper.convert_layouts(x_layouts)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2826376Z         log.debug("Missing virtual console keymap value %s converted from %s X layouts",
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2826683Z                   vc_keymap, x_layouts)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2826943Z     if not x_layouts:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2827295Z         x_layouts = localed_wrapper.convert_keymap(vc_keymap)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2827770Z         log.debug("Missing X layouts value %s converted from %s virtual console keymap",
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2828028Z                   x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2828124Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2828372Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2840712Z # Run a local bridge, web server, and browser for a particular Cockpit page.
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2841181Z # This is useful for integration into .desktop files, for systems which don't
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2841641Z # have cockpit.socket enabled. The web server and browser run in an unshared
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2842015Z # network namespace, and thus are totally isolated from everything else.
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2842215Z #
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2842403Z # Usage:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2842846Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 0
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2843316Z # For testing purposes one can also make cockpit-ws bind to all addresses
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2843724Z # (this is insecure and should not be used in prodcuction):
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2844205Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 1
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2844454Z #
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2844747Z set -eu
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2844891Z

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2845408Z WEBUI_REMOTE=0

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2845951Z do
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2846460Z     case "${option}"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2846727Z         in
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2846988Z         t)THEME_ID=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2847270Z         r)WEBUI_REMOTE=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2847631Z         *) echo "Usage: $0 [-t THEME_ID] [-r WEBUI_REMOTE]" >&2
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2847893Z        exit 1 ;;
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2848131Z     esac
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2848378Z done
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2848485Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2848854Z URL_PATH="/cockpit/@localhost/anaconda-webui/index.html"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2848981Z

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2851427Z # Function to setup user-related environment
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2851690Z setup_user_environment() {
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2852068Z     # PKEXEC_UID is the uid for "gnome-initial-setup" or "liveuser"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2852362Z     # depending on how the installer gets started.
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2852594Z     #
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2853058Z     # It's unset on non-live-images, so we just use the current user then (root)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2853349Z     if [ -n "${PKEXEC_UID:-}" ]; then
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2853729Z       # If PKEXEC_UID is set, use the user associated with it
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2854040Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(id -n -u "${PKEXEC_UID}")
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2854270Z     else
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2854737Z       # Try to find another user who is not root and has /usr/bin/bash as their shell
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2855297Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(grep '/usr/bin/bash' /etc/passwd | grep -v '^root' | cut -d: -f1 | head -n 1)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2855409Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2855800Z       # If no such user is found, use the current user running the script
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2856385Z       if [ -z "${INSTALLER_USER:-}" ]; then
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2856685Z         INSTALLER_USER=$(id -un)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2856941Z       fi
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2857175Z     fi
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2857282Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2857581Z     # Get the home directory of the selected user
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2857959Z     HOME_DIR=$(grep "^$INSTALLER_USER:" /etc/passwd | cut -d: -f6)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2858058Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2858603Z     # Check if the home directory exists, otherwise create it
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2858894Z     if [ ! -d "$HOME_DIR" ]; then
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2859310Z       echo "The home directory $HOME_DIR does not exist. Creating it." >&2
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2859767Z       mkdir -p "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2860067Z       chown "$INSTALLER_USER" "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2860324Z     else
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2860678Z       echo "Home directory $HOME_DIR already exists." >&2
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2860928Z     fi
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2861038Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2861379Z     # Return the user and home directory for further use
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2861658Z     echo "$INSTALLER_USER"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2861894Z }
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2862004Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2862269Z get_default_browser() {
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2862881Z     # Read the default browser from the anaconda runtime configuration file but wait till it's available
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2863319Z     # FIXME: Related to: https://issues.redhat.com/browse/INSTALLER-4223
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2863549Z     timeout=60
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2863764Z     elapsed=0
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2863867Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2864155Z     while [ ! -f /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf ]; do
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2864436Z         if [ "$elapsed" -ge "$timeout" ]; then
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2864835Z             echo "Timed out waiting for anaconda.conf to be available." >&2
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2865058Z             exit 1

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2904383Z # Check if gettext exists
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2904649Z if ! type gettext > /dev/null 2>&1; then
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2904986Z   # If not, create a dummy function that returns the input verbatim
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2905207Z   gettext() { printf '%s' "$1"; }
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2905398Z fi
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2905490Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2906479Z # returns OK if $1 contains literal string $2 (and isn't empty)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2906811Z strstr() {
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2907073Z     [ "${1##*"$2"*}" != "$1" ]
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2907515Z }
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2907621Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2907904Z # Run some action. Log its output.
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2908146Z action() {
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2908498Z     local STRING rc
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2908609Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2908858Z     STRING=$1
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2909135Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2909386Z     shift
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2909637Z     if "$@"; then
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2909925Z         success "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2910171Z     else
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2910443Z         failure "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2910687Z     fi
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2910939Z     rc=$?
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2911187Z     printf '\n'
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2911432Z     return $rc
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2911674Z }
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2911782Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2912180Z # verbose ->> very (very!) old bootup look (prior to RHL-6.0?)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2912477Z # color ->> default bootup look
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2912910Z # other ->> default bootup look without ANSI colors or positioning
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2913169Z BOOTUP=color
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2913445Z # Column to start "[  OK  ]" label in:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2913675Z RES_COL=60
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2913958Z # terminal sequence to move to that column:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2914220Z MOVE_TO_COL="printf \\033[${RES_COL}G"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2914607Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'success' (bright green):
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2914883Z SETCOLOR_SUCCESS="printf \\033[1;32m"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2915239Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'failure' (bright red):
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2915491Z SETCOLOR_FAILURE="printf \\033[1;31m"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2915884Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'warning' (bright yellow):
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2916417Z SETCOLOR_WARNING="printf \\033[1;33m"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2916770Z # Terminal sequence to reset to the default color:
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2917061Z SETCOLOR_NORMAL="printf \\033[0;39m"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2917168Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2917449Z # NOTE: /dev/ttyS* is serial console.
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2917836Z # 'not a tty' is reported when executed under systemd service units
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2918169Z # where /dev/null is associated with standard input
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2918272Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2918527Z test_tty=$(LC_ALL=C tty)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2918766Z case "$test_tty" in
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2919018Z     /dev/ttyS* | 'not a tty')
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2919255Z         BOOTUP=serial
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2919494Z         MOVE_TO_COL=
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2919744Z         SETCOLOR_SUCCESS=
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2920066Z         SETCOLOR_FAILURE=
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2920314Z         SETCOLOR_WARNING=
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2920560Z         SETCOLOR_NORMAL=
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2920789Z         ;;
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2921015Z esac
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2921114Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2921379Z # Log that something succeeded

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2922143Z     return 0
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2922370Z }
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2922472Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2922733Z # Log that something failed

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2923548Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_failure
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2923886Z     [ -x /bin/plymouth ] && /bin/plymouth --details
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2924146Z     return $rc
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2924382Z }
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2924507Z

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2925840Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "  OK  ")"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2926641Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2926931Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2927167Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2927388Z     return 0
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2927767Z }
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2927865Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2928087Z echo_failure() {

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2928565Z     printf '%s' "["

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2929349Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2929569Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2929796Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2930006Z     return 1
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2930219Z }

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2930534Z fscheck() {
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2930763Z     local fstype="$1"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2930988Z     local fs="$2"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2931220Z     local d="${3:-$2}"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2931468Z     umount "$fs" >/dev/null 2>&1 || :
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2931702Z     case "$fstype" in
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2931923Z         ext[432])
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2932152Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2932415Z             flock "$d" e2fsck -yfv "$fs" || :
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2932624Z             ;;
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2932846Z         vfat|msdos)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2933070Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2933323Z             flock "$d" fsck.fat -avVw "$fs"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2933539Z             ;;
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2933759Z         btrfs)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2934057Z             printf '# btrfs check -p --repair --force'
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2934385Z             flock "$d" btrfs check -p --repair --force "$fs"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2934598Z             ;;
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2934816Z         xfs)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2935067Z             printf '\n# xfs_repair -v'
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2935317Z             flock "$d" xfs_repair -v "$fs"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2935545Z             ;;
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2935768Z         f2fs)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2936210Z             printf '# fsck.f2fs -afy'
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2936591Z             flock "$d" fsck.f2fs -afy "$fs"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2936850Z             ;;
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2937089Z         *)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2937501Z             printf "\n\tERROR:   Filesystem checking for '%s' is not\n \
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2937790Z                 available.\n" "$fstype"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2938013Z     esac
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2938243Z }

2026-10-24T17:52:47.2965361Z mountPersistentHome() {
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2965663Z   # support label/uuid
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2965967Z   if [ "${homedev##LABEL=}" != "$homedev" ] ||
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2966542Z     [ "${homedev##UUID=}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2966867Z     homedev=$(blkid -o device -t "$homedev")
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2967113Z   fi
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2967222Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2967602Z   # if we're given a file rather than a blockdev, loop mount it
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2967898Z   if [ "${homedev##mtd}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2968330Z     # mtd devs don't have a block device but get magic-mounted with -t jffs2
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2968577Z     mountopts="-t jffs2"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2968834Z   elif [ ! -b "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2969169Z     if [ "${homedev##/run/initramfs/live}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2969556Z       action "Remounting live store r/w" mount -o remount,rw /run/initramfs/live
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2969748Z     fi
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2969986Z     homedev=$(losetup -f --show "$homedev")
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2970213Z   fi
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2970576Z   homefs=$(blkid -s TYPE -o value "$homedev" 2>/dev/null)
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2970696Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2970976Z   # if it's encrypted, we need to unlock it
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2971253Z   if [ "$homefs" = "crypto_LUKS" ]; then
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2971488Z     echo
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2971774Z     echo "Setting up encrypted /home device"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2972325Z     plymouth ask-for-password --command="cryptsetup luksOpen $homedev EncHome"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2972665Z     homedev=/dev/mapper/EncHome
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2972911Z   fi
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2973322Z   if [ "$homedev" ] && [ "${cmdline##* rd.skipfsck[= ]}" = "$cmdline" ]; then
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2973739Z     action "Checking home filesystem" fscheck "$homefs" "$homedev"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2973995Z   fi
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2974107Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2974369Z   # and finally do the mount
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2974685Z   mount ${mountopts:+$mountopts} "$homedev" /home
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2975087Z   # if we have /home under what's passed for persistent home, then
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2975502Z   # we should make that the real /home.  useful for mtd device on olpc
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2975829Z   [ -d /home/home ] && mount --bind /home/home /home
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2976524Z   [ -x /sbin/restorecon ] && /sbin/restorecon /home
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2976848Z   [ -d /home/liveuser ] && USERADDARGS="-M"
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2977093Z }
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2977206Z
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2977492Z cut=${cmdline##* persistenthome[= ]}
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2977759Z if [ ${#cut} -ne ${#cmdline} ]; then
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2977996Z   homedev=${cut%% *}
2026-10-24T17:52:47.2978346Z elif [ -e /run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img ]; then
